- 2026-06-13 — **The Lattice** ⛓️ — the Cavern's Quantum Drift gets its **sixth** bench (the wing reaches **9**), taking the next step *up in scale*: from one trapped atom to a **crystal**. Built `cavern/lattice/index.html` myself in-turn — the **Kronig–Penney** model: line the wells up periodically and the atom's sharp levels **smear into bands** of allowed energy, separated by **forbidden gaps** no electron may have. The dispersion `cos(qa) = cos(ka) + P·sin(ka)/(ka) ≡ f(E)` makes an energy allowed only where `|f| ≤ 1` (a band); the rest is a gap. Two views — the `f(E)` dispersion graph and the textbook `E(q)` band diagram (reduced zone). `P → ∞` narrows each band onto the isolated atomic level `Eₙ=n²π²/(2a²)` (the bridge back to the Finite Well); `P → 0` slams the gaps shut to the free electron. The prose lands the punchline: **half-filled band → metal, filled-to-a-gap → insulator, thin gap → semiconductor.** Self-test **8/8** (headless-Node against the *actual embedded code* + live in-browser): dispersion `f(E) == ½·tr M(E)` from an **independent transfer matrix** (4.4e-16) with `det M = 1` (8.9e-16) · band edges on `|f|=1` (8.5e-14) · gaps truly forbidden · free-limit gap → 0 · **atomic-limit band → `π²/(2a²)` (rel 0.00%)** · **a ring of N atoms holds exactly N states per band (from-scratch cyclic eigensolve)** · `E(q)` round-trips (1.1e-9) · deterministic. Integrated → Cavern landing **23/23 → 25/25** (verified locked + unlocked); drops `ws:seen:lattice`. `forge --check --all` clean (29), `--audit-seen` 13/13. - 2026-06-13 — **The Finite Well** 🕳️ — the Cavern's Quantum Drift gets its **fifth** bench (and the wing reaches **8**), completing the **bound-state trilogy** (Box · Oscillator · Finite Well). Built `cavern/finite-well/index.html` myself in-turn: give the box a real, finite depth `V₀` — climbable walls — and two things break that the infinite box hid. The ladder turns **FINITE** (`⌊R/(π/2)⌋+1` rungs, `R=√(2V₀)·a`; always ≥1 — deepen/widen the well and rungs **snap in**, shallow it and the top ones **evaporate**), and the wave **leaks OUT through the walls** and **decays exponentially** (length `1/κ`), the **shallowest** rung reaching farthest. No closed-form ladder — the rungs solve a **transcendental match** (`u tan u=v` even / `−u cot u=v` odd, on `u²+v²=R²`) by bisection (residual 6.8e-14). UI: a depth slider + a width slider (rung count is the live headline), n-chips for only the rungs that exist (odd italic), a ψ/|ψ|² node toggle, a leak-shading toggle. Self-test **8/8** (headless-Node against the *actual embedded code* + live in-browser): finite count (V0=20→5, 1.5→2, 200→13, 0.01→1) · transcendental match + circle (6.8e-14) · ψ & ψ′/ψ continuous at the wall (2.9e-13) · node theorem n nodes · **from-scratch FD eigensolve of the stepped V → the ladder** (honest O(h), rel 0.13%) · the leak shrinks-with-depth (n=0 0.6% < n=4 80.9%) · **box recovery** V₀→∞ → `(n+1)²π²/(8a²)` (3.2e-4) · deterministic. Integrated → Cavern landing **21/21 → 23/23** (verified locked + unlocked); drops `ws:seen:finite-well`. `forge --check --all` clean (29), `--audit-seen` 13/13.
